- **thermal-overload-mitigation**: Senior power-engineer playbook for overloaded lines and transformers. Use whenever power-flow or contingency studies show loading above ratings — including escalations from a tool skill where branch loading exceeds 100% of its rating. Triggers on "line overloaded", "transformer above rating", "relieve congestion", or "redispatch to unload". Gives an operationally credible sequence of redispatch, topology, phase-shifter, and reinforcement actions rather than only flagging the overload. ([Power-Agent/PowerSkills](https://github.com/Power-Agent/PowerSkills/tree/HEAD/powerskills-engineering/skills/thermal-overload-mitigation) · ★81 · 2 copies)

- **thermal-pde-audit**: 将结构化或中英文自然语言的一维热传导任务转化为带物理审计的可复现实验，联合解析解、显式有限差分、UnitaryLab Schrödingerization CPU/壁仞 GPU 量子线路仿真、torch.supa 与项目自研 SUPA 归约、误差分层和证据报告。适用于热传导 PDE 仿真、CPU/GPU 对照、量子方程求解演示、结果复核与竞赛实验复现。 ([HNNUYUXUAN/thermal-pde-audit](https://github.com/HNNUYUXUAN/thermal-pde-audit/tree/HEAD/skills/thermal-pde-audit) · 4 copies)
- **thermal-porosity**: Predict layer-wise porosity in laser powder bed fusion from in-situ thermal (pyrometry) image sequences — empty-frame filtering, CT pore alignment and ESD thresholds, pore-count regression and 16x16 porosity localization with interpretable thermal-history baselines and the paper's CNN / video-transformer models, scored by RMSE, R² and IoU. Based on ThermoPore (Barati Farimani Lab with Sandia). Use for in-situ monitoring data, porosity digital twins, CT-to-thermal correlation, or part-qualification studies. ([BaratiLab/mechanical-engineering-skills](https://github.com/BaratiLab/mechanical-engineering-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/thermal-porosity) · ★18)
- **thermal-predictor**: Utilise PoleSpectrum pour predire la charge thermique avant execution et ajuster la charge pour garantir T_reelle &lt; 70 degC. Remplace le M5 reactif actuel par un M5 proactif. ([gerivdb/SKILLS](https://github.com/gerivdb/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/thermal-predictor) · ★1)
